- **deterministic-controller**: Deterministic, evidence-gated controller templates for OpenClaw (HEARTBEAT/ACTIVITIES + sprint template + poll cron payload). Docs-only: installs no services and makes no config changes by itself. ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/tree/HEAD/data/deterministic-controller) · ★21 · 5 copies)
- **deterministic-core**: Keep any consequential output path reproducible bit-for-bit and tamper-evident — no floats in the decision path, canonical typed serialization, SHA-256 sealing, and a determinism check. Use this whenever you build or review code that produces a verdict, score, decision, risk number, classification, or any result that will be audited, sealed, hashed, or relied on as evidence; whenever a hash changes between runs and shouldn't; whenever floating-point arithmetic sits anywhere near a result that must be exactly reproducible; and whenever someone says "reproducible", "deterministic", "seal", "cha… ([annatchijova/SKILLS](https://github.com/annatchijova/SKILLS/tree/HEAD/deterministic-core) · ★9 · 4 copies)
- **deterministic-core-llm-judgment**: Build agents where the deterministic duties are the load-bearing backbone (always execute, reliable, auditable) and the LLM only adds an optional judgment layer on top — ranking, curation, synthesis. The agent must do its core work with the LLM layer off; the LLM only makes the work better. Use when designing or building an autonomous agent, a recurring agent loop, or any system where reliability matters more than cleverness. ([jcdavis131/cursor-agent-skills](https://github.com/jcdavis131/cursor-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/deterministic-core-llm-judgment))
- **deterministic-core-llm-surface-pattern**: Apply the 95/5 architecture pattern when designing or reviewing an AI-powered feature. Keep roughly 95% of the system as deterministic, testable, versioned code and confine the LLM to the thin surface where genuine judgment about ambiguous natural language is actually required. Use this skill whenever someone is about to put math, business rules, state management, or control flow inside a prompt, when an AI feature feels unreliable or hard to test, or when reviewing an agent or product design to decide where the model boundary should sit. ([mizcausevic-dev/deterministic-core-llm-surface-pattern](https://github.com/mizcausevic-dev/deterministic-core-llm-surface-pattern/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
